# dcreborn -- Reborn Costume identities, read by ReBalanceOfSouls\dcreborn.dll.
# The index is the VISIBLE picker rank (mission_only rows take none), not cos_NN.
#
# name <chara> <visible index> <NAME IN CAPS> -- must fit the character's own EN name
#
# tint <chara> <visible index> <r> <g> <b> <saturation> <effect...>
# Saturation is applied after the modulate, so 0 turns the ash grey and a
# modulate above 1 lifts it toward snow white.
tint 40 0 1.80 1.80 1.80 0.0 P012_com_ash P012_com_evo_haineko P012_cs_sp1_00 P012_cs_sp1_01 P012_cs_sp2_evo_00
#
# tintown <chara> <visible index> <r> <g> <b> <saturation> <effect...>
# A SHARED effect every fighter uses, tinted only when it was spawned by an
# object that also spawned this costume's own effects (the fighter, or its
# cutscene). The Awakening's common aura and its transformation burst:
tintown 40 0 1.80 1.80 1.80 0.0 COM_tm_EvolveAura00 COM_sp_CsTrnEvo_00
#
# tintkick <chara> <visible index> <r> <g> <b> <saturation> <effect> <trigger...>
# One TIMELINE's look: applied when the game kicks one of these triggers on
# an instance of the effect. The Awakening's sword ash is P012_com_ash
# trigger 28 (T28_WepTrn), bound to weapon0_0. That one timeline carries the
# blade's glow AND the ash it dissolves into, so the green is held only for
# the glow (@seconds) and then hands back to the white rule above. Trigger 29
# (T29_WepTrn2) is ash and mist only, and stays white. Saturation stays 1.
# (removed 2026-10-05: the green could not be confined to the glow -- particles
# emitted while it was green kept the colour for seconds. The ash stays white.)
# tintkick 12 5 0.45 1.70 0.60 1.0 P012_com_ash 28 @0.25
#
# --- Aaroniero Aaruruerie on Kaien Shiba (pl031): cos_04 Masked, cos_05 Unmasked.
# cos_03 is mission-only and takes no rank, so these are visible 3 and 4.
# Kaien's water -- Nejibana, the Water Aura, his Kikon and Awakening -- in
# Shunsui's poison violet. Aimed at P024_com_attack's own ramps (hot pink ->
# magenta -> violet, mean RGB 162,140,198) from Kaien's (mean 95,169,207):
# red up, green well down, blue held, saturation lifted so it reads as poison
# rather than lilac. A modulate cannot copy his ramps exactly -- only a file
# recolour can, and that would change every Kaien costume.
tint 41 0 1.70 0.55 1.10 1.25 P031_com_wep P031_com_wep_matoi P031_Attack00 P031_cs_sp1_00 P031_cs_sp2_evo_00 P031_cs_trn_evo_00 P031_cs_app_01
tint 41 1 1.70 0.55 1.10 1.25 P031_com_wep P031_com_wep_matoi P031_Attack00 P031_cs_sp1_00 P031_cs_sp2_evo_00 P031_cs_trn_evo_00 P031_cs_app_01
#
# soundkey <chara> <visible index> <match> <key>
# While this costume's fighter loads, a model part whose name contains <match>
# looks up Script/cos_soundstate.fsv by <key> instead -- rows match by
# SUBSTRING, so <key> reaches a row of its own. Aaroniero's two slots share
# en037_cos00_00 with Kaien's Arrancar Garb, which keeps Kaien's voice; the
# row dcrb_pl031 adds VoiceCos=Aaroniero to that body's own switches.
soundkey 41 0 en037_cos00 dcrb_pl031_04
soundkey 41 1 en037_cos00 dcrb_pl031_05
#
#
# Aaroniero's spiritual burst -- the shared light-blue flame his Spiritual
# Pressure Moves, Hakugeki and heavy attacks set off (COM_sp_Atk_lb) -- in RED,
# only when his fighter spawns it (tintown). Blue is lowered to nothing and red
# lifted, so the white-hot core stays bright and the flame reads as red.
tintown 41 0 1.80 0.35 0.30 1.40 COM_sp_Atk_lb
tintown 41 1 1.80 0.35 0.30 1.40 COM_sp_Atk_lb
#
# ...and the SLASHES that set it off, in PURPLE. They are white layers of
# P031_com_wep (rotating slashes 1/2, the Spiritual Pressure slash 15), and the
# water rule above turns white pink-red; this maps white to violet instead.
tintkick 41 0 0.85 0.40 1.30 1.20 P031_com_wep 1 2 15
tintkick 41 1 0.85 0.40 1.30 1.20 P031_com_wep 1 2 15
# Rangiku Halloween (visible costume 5): her ash cycles the colour wheel every 2 s (dcreborn 0.33.3)
tintrainbow 12 5 2.0 1.60 1.30 P012_com_ash P012_com_evo_haineko P012_cs_sp1_00 P012_cs_sp1_01 P012_cs_sp2_evo_00
